MODEL_NAME,\\n    LORA_RANK,\\n    GATE_HIDDEN_DIM,\\n    MAX_TURN_DISTANCE,\\n    TURN_EMBED_DIM,\\n)\\n\\n\\nclass TurnEmbedding(nn.Module):\\n\\n    def __init__(self):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        half_dim = TURN_EMBED_DIM // 2\\n        num_buckets = MAX_TURN_DISTANCE + 1\\n        self.distance_embed = nn.Embedding(num_buckets, half_dim)\\n        self.index_embed = nn.Embedding(num_buckets, half_dim)\\n\\n    def forward(self, turn_distance, turn_index):\\n        td = turn_distance.clamp(0, MAX_TURN_DISTANCE)\\n        ti = turn_index.clamp(0, MAX_TURN_DISTANCE)\\n        return torch.cat([self.distance_embed(td), self.index_embed(ti)], dim=-1)\\n\\n\\nclass GateNetwork(nn.Module):\\n\\n    def __init__(self, hidden_dim, turn_embedding, init_bias=5.0):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.down_proj = nn.Linear(hidden_dim, LORA_RANK, bias=False)\\n        self.turn_embedding = turn_embedding\\n        self.fc1 = nn.Linear(LORA_RANK + TURN_EMBED_DIM, GATE_HIDDEN_DIM)\\n        self.fc2 = nn.Linear(GATE_HIDDEN_DIM, 1)\\n        self.fc2.bias.data.fill_(init_bias)\\n\\n    def forward(self, hidden_state, turn_features):\\n        down = self.down_proj(hidden_state)\\n        turn_emb = self.turn_embedding(\\n            turn_features[\\\"turn_distance\\\"],\\n            turn_features[\\\"turn_index\\\"],\\n        )\\n        while turn_emb.dim() < down.dim():\\n            turn_emb = turn_emb.unsqueeze(0)\\n        if turn_emb.shape[0] == 1 and down.shape[0] > 1:\\n            turn_emb = turn_emb.expand(down.shape[0], -1, -1)\\n        combined = torch.cat([down, turn_emb], dim=-1)\\n        return torch.sigmoid(self.fc2(torch.relu(self.fc1(combined))))\\n\\n\\nclass UniformGateNetwork(nn.Module):\\n\\n    def __init__(self, hidden_dim, init_bias=5.0):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.down_proj = nn.Linear(hidden_dim, LORA_RANK, bias=False)\\n        self.constant_vector = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(TURN_EMBED_DIM))\\n        self.fc1 = nn.Linear(LORA_RANK + TURN_EMBED_DIM, GATE_HIDDEN_DIM)\\n        self.fc2 = nn.Linear(GATE_HIDDEN_DIM, 1)\\n        self.fc2.bias.data.fill_(init_bias)\\n\\n    def forward(self, hidden_state):\\n        h = self.down_proj(hidden_state)\\n        c = self.constant_vector.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0).expand(\\n            hidden_state.size(0), hidden_state.size(1), -1\\n        )\\n        combined = torch.cat([h, c], dim=-1)\\n        return torch.sigmoid(self.fc2(torch.relu(self.fc1(combined))))\\n\\n\\nclass ThreadGatedModel(nn.Module):\\n\\n    def __init__(self, model_name=MODEL_NAME, gate_type=\\\"threadgate\\\", init_bias=2.0, device_map=\\\"auto\\\"):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.gate_type = gate_type\\n\\n        self.base_model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(\\n            model_name, torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16, device_map=device_map,\\n        )\\n        for param in self.base_model.parameters():\\n            param.requires_grad = False\\n\\n        hidden_dim = self.base_model.config.hidden_size\\n        num_layers = self.base_model.config.num_hidden_layers\\n        self.num_layers = num_layers\\n\\n        if gate_type == \\\"none\\\":\\n            self.gates = None\\n        elif gate_type == \\\"threadgate\\\":\\n            self.turn_embedding = TurnEmbedding()\\n            self.gates = nn.ModuleList([\\n                GateNetwork(hidden_dim, self.turn_embedding, init_bias)\\n                for _ in range(num_layers)\\n            ])\\n        else:\\n            self.gates = nn.ModuleList([\\n                UniformGateNetwork(hidden_dim, init_bias)\\n                for _ in range(num_layers)\\n            ])\\n\\n        if self.gates is not None:\\n            for i, layer in enumerate(self.base_model.model.layers):\\n                device = next(layer.parameters()).device\\n                self.gates[i].to(device=device, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\\n            if gate_type == \\\"threadgate\\\":\\n                device = next(self.gates[0].parameters()).device\\n                self.turn_embedding.to(device=device, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\\n\\n        self._gate_values = {}\\n        self._gate_values_with_grad = {}\\n        self._current_turn_features = None\\n\\n        self._hooks = []\\n        if self.gate_type != \\\"none\\\":\\n            for i, layer in enumerate(self.base_model.model.layers):\\n                self._hooks.append(layer.register_forward_hook(self._make_hook(i)))\\n\\n    def _make_hook(self, layer_idx):\\n        def mean_gar)\\n\\n    elif mode == \\\"smooth_saturation\\\":\\n        all_gar = []\\n        for g in gar_per_turn:\\n            if not isinstance(g, torch.Tensor):\\n                g = torch.tensor(g, dtype=torch.float32)\\n            all_gar.append(g)\\n        mean_gar = torch.stack(all_gar).mean()\\n        return -target * torch.tanh(mean_gar / target)\\n\\n    elif mode == \\\"mse_target\\\":\\n        # Bidirectional MSE loss: penalizes GAR both above and below target\\n        all_gar = []\\n        for g in gar_per_turn:\\n            if not isinstance(g, torch.Tensor):\\n                g = torch.tensor(g, dtype=torch.float32)\\n            all_gar.append(g)\\n        mean_gar = torch.stack(all_gar).mean()\\n        return (mean_gar - init_bias=5.0)\\n            for _ in range(num_layers)\\n        ])\\n\\n    gate_path = os.path.join(checkpoint_dir, \\\"gate_params.pt\\\")\\n    if not os.path.exists(gate_path):\\n        raise     def hook(module, input, output):\\n            if gate_type == \\\"threadgate\\\" and turn_features_ref[0] is None:\\n                return output\\n\\n            layer_input = input[0]\\n Now I'll write the script. The key points from reading the codebase:

1. **Model loading**: Use `ThreadGatedModel` which internally creates the base model + gates + hooks. Then load LoRA via `peft` and gate params from `gate_params.pt`.
2. **GAR computation**: Use `GARMeasurer(hf_model, measure_only=False)` with `.measure()` context + `output_attentions=True`, then `compute_gar_differentiable` per turn, then `compute_gar_loss(mode="mse_target", target=0.5)`.
3. **Gate params**: Located in `model.gates` (ModuleList of 28 GateNetworks) + `model.turn_embedding`.
4. **v7 config**: `gar_loss_mode=mse_target`, `gar_target=0.5`, `lambda_gar=1.0`, `init_bias=1.5`.

model(input_ids, labels=labels, turn_features=turn_features)\n    l_ntp = outputs_ntp.loss\n    l_ntp.backward()\n    grads_ntp = collect_gate_grads(model)\n    ntp_loss_val = l_ntp.item()\n\n    # --- GAR gradient ---\n    model.zero_grad()\n    with measurer.measure():\n        outputs_gar = model(\n            input_ids,\n            turn_features=turn_features,\n            output_attentions=True,\n        )\n\n    gar_per_turn = []\n    for pos in assistant_pos:\n        gar_val = measurer.compute_gar_differentiable(goal_pos, [pos])\n        gar_per_turn.append(gar_val)\n\n    l_gar = compute_gar_loss(gar_per_turn, epsilon=gar_epsilon, mode=gar_loss_mode, target=gar_target)\n    l_gar.backward()\n    grads_gar = collect_gate_grads(model)\n    gar_loss_val = l_gar.item()\n\n    model.zero_grad()\n\n    metrics = compute_conflict_metrics(grads_ntp, grads_gar)\n\n    return metrics, ntp_loss_val, gar_loss_val\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Gradient conflict analysis between NTP and GAR losses on gate parameters\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--checkpoint-dir\", type=str, default=\"/root/turn_residual_gating/runs/phase2_tg_s42_v7\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--data-dir\", type=str, default=\"/root/turn_residual_gating/data/ultrachat_8k\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--model\", type=str, default=MODEL_NAME)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-samples\", type=int, default=10)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--max-seq-len\", type=int, default=2048)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--seed\", type=int, default=42)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--output\", type=str, default=\"/root/turn_residual_gating/results/gradient_conflict_analysis.json\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--gar-loss-mode\", type=str, default=\"mse_target\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--gar-target\", type=float, default=0.5)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--gar-epsilon\", type=float, default=0.001)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--init-bias\", type=float, default=1.5)\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    set_seed(args.seed)\n\n    print(f\"Loading model: {args.model}\")\n    print(f\"Checkpoint: {args.checkpoint_dir}\")\n\n    model = ThreadGatedModel(\n        model_name=args.model,\n        gate_type=\"threadgate\",\n        init_bias=args.init_bias,\n        device_map=\"auto\",\n    )\n\n    lora_path = os.path.join(args.checkpoint_dir, \"lora\")\n    if os.path.exists(lora_path):\n        print(f\"Loading LoRA from {lora_path}\")\n        model.base_model = PeftModel.from_pretrained(\n            model.base_model, lora_path, is_trainable=False\n        )\n\n    gate_path = os.path.join(args.checkpoint_dir, \"gate_params.pt\")\n    if os.path.exists(gate_path):\n        print(f\"Loading gate params from {gate_path}\")\n        gate_state = torch.load(gate_path, map_location=\"cpu\", weights_only=True)\n        model.gates.load_state_dict(gate_state[\"gates\"])\n        if \"turn_embedding\" in gate_state:\n            model.turn_embedding.load_state_dict(gate_state[\"turn_embedding\"])\n\n    device = next(model.base_model.parameters()).device\n    for i in range(len(model.gates)):\n        model.gates[i].to(device=device, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\n    model.turn_embedding.to(device=device, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\n\n    for p in model.gates.parameters():\n        p.requires_grad_(True)\n    for p in model.turn_embedding.parameters():\n        p.requires_grad_(True)\n\n    hf_model = model.base_model\n    if hasattr(hf_model, \"base_model\"):\n        inner = hf_model.base_model\n        if hasattr(inner, \"model\") and hasattr(inner.model, \"model\"):\n            raw_hf = inner.model\n        else:\n            raw_hf = hf_model\n    else:\n        raw_hf = hf_model\n    measurer = GARMeasurer(raw_hf, measure_only=False)\n\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(args.model)\n\n    print(f\"Loading data from {args.data_dir}\")\n    ds = load_from_disk(args.data_dir)\n\n    num_layers = model.num_layers\n    print(f\"Model layers: {num_layers}\")\n    print(f\"GAR loss mode: {args.gar_loss_mode}, target: {args.gar_target}\")\n    print(f\"Analyzing {args.num_samples} samples...\")\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n\n    all_metrics = []\n    all_ntp_losses = []\n    all_gar_losses = []\n    sample_idx = 0\n    analyzed = 0\n\n    while analyzed < args.num_samples and sample_idx < len(ds):\n        sample = prepare_sample(ds, sample_idx, tokenizer, args.max_seq_len, device)\n        sample_idx += 1\n\n        if not sample[\"valid\"]:\n            continue\n\n        print(f\"\\nSample {analyzed + 1}/{args.num_samples} (dataset idx {sample_idx - 1})\")\n        metrics, ntp_loss, gar_loss = analyze_sample(\n            model, measurer, sample,\n            args.gar_loss_mode, args.gar_target, args.gar_epsilon,\n        )\n        all_metrics.append(metrics)\n        all_ntp_losses.append(ntp_loss)\n        all_gar_losses.append(gar_loss)\n        analyzed += 1\n\n        layer_keys = sorted(k for k in metrics if isinstance(k, int))\n        cos_vals = [metrics[k][\"cosine_similarity\"] for k in layer_keys]\n        ratio_vals = [metrics[k][\"magnitude_ratio\"] for k in layer_keys]\n        print(f\"  NTP loss: {ntp_loss:.4f}, GAR loss: {gar_loss:.6f}\")\n        print(f\"  Avg cos_sim: {np.mean(cos_vals):.4f}, Avg mag_ratio: {np.median(ratio_vals):.1f}\")\n\n    if not all_metrics:\n        print(\"No valid samples found.\")\n        return\n\n    # Aggregate per-layer stats\n    per_layer_results = {}\n    for layer_idx in range(num_layers):\n        cos_vals = [m[layer_idx][\"cosine_similarity\"] for m in all_metrics if layer_idx in m]\n        ratio_vals = [m[layer_idx][\"magnitude_ratio\"] for m in all_metrics if layer_idx in m]\n        norm_ntp_vals = [m[layer_idx][\"norm_ntp\"] for m in all_metrics if layer_idx in m]\n        norm_gar_vals = [m[layer_idx][\"norm_gar\"] for m in all_metrics if layer_idx in m]\n\n        per_layer_results[f\"layer_{layer_idx}\"] = {\n            \"cosine_similarity_mean\": float(np.mean(cos_vals)),\n            \"cosine_similarity_std\": float(np.std(cos_vals)),\n            \"magnitude_ratio_mean\": float(np.mean(ratio_vals)),\n            \"magnitude_ratio_median\": float(np.median(ratio_vals)),\n            \"norm_ntp_mean\": float(np.mean(norm_ntp_vals)),\n            \"norm_gar_mean\": float(np.mean(norm_gar_vals)),\n        }\n\n    te_results = None\n    te_cos = [m[\"turn_embedding\"][\"cosine_similarity\"] for m in all_metrics if \"turn_embedding\" in m]\n    if te_cos:\n        te_ratio = [m[\"turn_embedding\"][\"magnitude_ratio\"] for m in all_metrics if \"turn_embedding\" in m]\n        te_results = {\n            \"cosine_similarity_mean\": float(np.mean(te_cos)),\n            \"cosine_similarity_std\": float(np.std(te_cos)),\n            \"magnitude_ratio_mean\": float(np.mean(te_ratio)),\n            \"magnitude_ratio_median\": float(np.median(te_ratio)),\n        }\n\n    all_cos = [per_layer_results[f\"layer_{i}\"][\"cosine_similarity_mean\"] for i in range(num_layers)]\n    all_ratios = [per_layer_results[f\"layer_{i}\"][\"magnitude_ratio_median\"] for i in range(num_layers)]\n    negative_layers = sum(1 for c in all_cos if c < 0)\n    strong_negative = sum(1 for c in all_cos if c < -0.3)\n\n    summary = {\n        \"num_samples\": len(all_metrics),\n        \"avg_ntp_loss\": float(np.mean(all_ntp_losses)),\n        \"avg_gar_loss\": float(np.mean(all_gar_losses)),\n        \"overall_cos_sim_mean\": float(np.mean(all_cos)),\n        \"overall_cos_sim_std\": float(np.std(all_cos)),\n        \"overall_magnitude_ratio_median\": float(np.median(all_ratios)),\n        \"overall_magnitude_ratio_mean\": float(np.mean(all_ratios)),\n        \"negative_cos_layers\": negative_layers,\n        \"strong_negative_cos_layers\": strong_negative,\n        \"total_layers\": num_layers,\n        \"conflict_fraction\": negative_layers / num_layers,\n    }\n\n    results = {\n        \"config\": {\n            \"checkpoint_dir\": args.checkpoint_dir,\n            \"gar_loss_mode\": args.gar_loss_mode,\n            \"gar_target\": args.gar_target,\n            \"num_samples\": args.num_samples,\n            \"max_seq_len\": args.max_seq_len,\n            \"seed\": args.seed,\n        },\n        \"summary\": summary,\n        \"per_layer\": per_layer_results,\n        \"turn_embedding\": te_results,\n    }\n\n    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(args.output), exist_ok=True)\n    with open(args.output, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"\\nResults saved to {args.output}\")\n\n    # Print table\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*90}\")\n    print(f\"Gradient Conflict Analysis: NTP vs GAR on Gate Parameters\")\n    print(f\"{'='*90}\")\n    print(f\"Checkpoint: {args.checkpoint_dir}\")\n    print(f\"Samples: {len(all_metrics)}, GAR mode: {args.gar_loss_mode}, target: {args.gar_target}\")\n    print(f\"Avg NTP loss: {summary['avg_ntp_loss']:.4f}, Avg GAR loss: {summary['avg_gar_loss']:.6f}\")\n    print(f\"\\n{'Layer':<10} {'cos_sim':>10} {'std':>8} {'||∇NTP||':>12} {'||∇GAR||':>12} {'ratio':>10}\")\n    print(\"-\" * 62)\n\n    for i in range(num_layers):\n        lr = per_layer_results[f\"layer_{i}\"]\n        conflict_marker = \" <--\" if lr[\"cosine_similarity_mean\"] < -0.3 else \"\"\n        print(\n            f\"  L{i:<6} {lr['cosine_similarity_mean']:>10.4f} {lr['cosine_similarity_std']:>8.4f} \"\n            f\"{lr['norm_ntp_mean']:>12.6f} {lr['norm_gar_mean']:>12.6f} \"\n            f\"{lr['magnitude_ratio_median']:>10.1f}{conflict_marker}\"\n        )\n\n    if te_results:\n        print(\n            f\"  {'TurnEmb':<8} {te_results['cosine_similarity_mean']:>10.4f} \"\n            f\"{te_results['cosine_similarity_std']:>8.4f} \"\n            f\"{'':>12} {'':>12} {te_results['magnitude_ratio_median']:>10.1f}\"\n        )\n\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*70}\")\n    print(f\"Summary:\")\n    print(f\"  Overall cos_sim: {summary['overall_cos_sim_mean']:.4f} +/- {summary['overall_cos_sim_std']:.4f}\")\n    print(f\"  Layers with negative cos_sim: {negative_layers}/{num_layers} ({summary['conflict_fraction']*100:.1f}%)\")\n    print(f\"  Layers with strong conflict (cos < -0.3): {strong_negative}/{num_layers}\")\n    print(f\"  Median magnitude ratio (||∇NTP|| / ||∇GAR||): {summary['overall_magnitude_ratio_median']:.1f}\")\n\n    if summary[\"conflict_fraction\"] > 0.5:\n        print(f\"\\n  CONCLUSION: Widespread gradient conflict detected.\")\n        print(f\"  NTP and GAR losses push gate params in opposing directions.\")\n    elif summary[\"conflict_fraction\"] > 0.2:\n        print(f\"\\n  CONCLUSION: Partial gradient conflict in {negative_layers} layers.\")\n    else:\n        print(f\"\\n  CONCLUSION: Minimal gradient conflict between NTP and GAR.\")\n\n    if summary[\"overall_magnitude_ratio_median\"] > 10:\n        print(f\"  NTP gradient dominates by {summary['overall_magnitude_ratio_median']:.0f}x, GAR signal likely overwhelmed.\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\nSCRIPT_EOF"

_source line 63_

脚本已部署到 `/root/turn_residual_gating/src/gradient_conflict_analysis.py`，`--help` 验证通过。

脚本逻辑：
- 加载 v7 checkpoint（ThreadGatedModel + LoRA + gate_params.pt）
- 遍历 ultrachat 样本，对每个样本分别 backward NTP loss 和 GAR loss（`mse_target`, target=0.5）
- 两次 backward 之间 `model.zero_grad()` 清零
- 收集 28 层 gate 参数梯度，逐层计算 cosine similarity 和 magnitude ratio
- 输出 JSON 到 `results/gradient_conflict_analysis.json` + 打印逐层分析表

默认参数匹配 v7 训练配置（`init_bias=1.5`, `gar_target=0.5`, `gar_loss_mode=mse_target`）。GPU 空闲后直接运行即可。

- 两次 backward 之间 `model.zero_grad()` 清零
- 收集 28 层 gate 参数梯度，逐层计算 cosine similarity 和 magnitude ratio
- 输出 JSON 到 `results/gradient_conflict_analysis.json` + 打印逐层分析表

默认参数匹配 v7 训练配置（`init_bias=1.5`, `gar_target=0.5`, `gar_loss_mode=mse_target`）。GPU 空闲后直接运行即可。
